<script nonce="9Vq/Kfn10b5K09mw5QdpHg==">
  (function() {
    if (window.NeuralBackground) return;

    class NeuralBackground {
      constructor() {
        this.canvas = document.getElementById('neural-canvas-primary');
        if (!this.canvas) return;

        this.ctx = this.canvas.getContext('2d');
        this.nodes = [];
        this.connections = [];
        this.animationId = null;
        this.mouse = { x: 0, y: 0 };

        this.init();
        this.animate();
        this.bindEvents();
      }

      init() {
        this.resize();
        this.createNodes();
        this.createConnections();
      }

      resize() {
        const rect = this.canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
        this.canvas.width = rect.width * window.devicePixelRatio;
        this.canvas.height = rect.height * window.devicePixelRatio;
        this.ctx.scale(window.devicePixelRatio, window.devicePixelRatio);
        this.canvas.style.width = rect.width + 'px';
        this.canvas.style.height = rect.height + 'px';
      }

      createNodes() {
        this.nodes = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < 15; i++) {
          this.nodes.push({
            x: Math.random() * this.canvas.offsetWidth,
            y: Math.random() * this.canvas.offsetHeight,
            vx: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5,
            vy: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5,
            radius: Math.random() * 3 + 1,
            alpha: Math.random() * 0.5 + 0.2
          });
        }
      }

      createConnections() {
        this.connections = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < this.nodes.length; i++) {
          for (let j = i + 1; j < this.nodes.length; j++) {
            const dx = this.nodes[i].x - this.nodes[j].x;
            const dy = this.nodes[i].y - this.nodes[j].y;
            const distance = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);

            if (distance < 150) {
              this.connections.push({
                from: i,
                to: j,
                alpha: (150 - distance) / 150 * 0.3
              });
            }
          }
        }
      }

      animate() {
        this.ctx.clearRect(0, 0, this.canvas.offsetWidth, this.canvas.offsetHeight);

        // Update nodes
        this.nodes.forEach(node => {
          node.x += node.vx;
          node.y += node.vy;

          // Bounce off edges
          if (node.x <= 0 || node.x >= this.canvas.offsetWidth) node.vx *= -1;
          if (node.y <= 0 || node.y >= this.canvas.offsetHeight) node.vy *= -1;

          // Mouse interaction
          const dx = node.x - this.mouse.x;
          const dy = node.y - this.mouse.y;
          const distance = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
          if (distance < 100) {
            node.vx += dx * 0.0001;
            node.vy += dy * 0.0001;
          }

          // Draw node
          this.ctx.beginPath();
          this.ctx.arc(node.x, node.y, node.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          this.ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(0, 212, 255, ${node.alpha})`;
          this.ctx.fill();
        });

        // Draw connections
        this.connections.forEach(conn => {
          const from = this.nodes[conn.from];
          const to = this.nodes[conn.to];

          this.ctx.beginPath();
          this.ctx.moveTo(from.x, from.y);
          this.ctx.lineTo(to.x, to.y);
          this.ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(0, 255, 157, ${conn.alpha})`;
          this.ctx.lineWidth = 0.5;
          this.ctx.stroke();
        });

        this.animationId = requestAnimationFrame(() => this.animate());
      }

      bindEvents() {
        const handleMouseMove = (e) => {
          const rect = this.canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
          this.mouse.x = e.clientX - rect.left;
          this.mouse.y = e.clientY - rect.top;
        };

        const handleResize = () => {
          this.resize();
          this.createNodes();
        };

        window.addEventListener('mousemove', handleMouseMove);
        window.addEventListener('resize', handleResize);

        // Cleanup on page unload
        window.addEventListener('beforeunload', () => {
          if (this.animationId) {
            cancelAnimationFrame(this.animationId);
          }
        });
      }
    }

    // Initialize when DOM is ready
    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
      document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
         window.NeuralBackground = new NeuralBackground();
      });
    } else {
       window.NeuralBackground = new NeuralBackground();
    }
  })();

// Contador regressivo de 12 minutos que reinicia
function startCountdown() {
  let minutes = 11;
  let seconds = 59;
  const countdownEl = document.getElementById("countdown");
  
  if (!countdownEl) return;

  setInterval(() => {
    seconds--;
    if (seconds < 0) {
      seconds = 59;
      minutes--;
      if (minutes < 0) {
        minutes = 11;
        seconds = 59;
      }
    }
    countdownEl.textContent = `${minutes.toString().padStart(2,"0")}:${seconds.toString().padStart(2,"0")}`;
  }, 1000);
}
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", startCountdown);

// Carregamento dinâmico do script de modais apenas quando necessário
function loadModalScripts() {
  if (!window.getNexoState || window.getNexoState.scriptsLoaded || window.getNexoState.scriptsLoading) return;
  
  window.getNexoState.scriptsLoading = true;
  
  // Carrega o script de modais dinamicamente
  const script = document.createElement('script');
  script.src = '/custom/js/modals-demo.js';
  script.defer = true;
  if (nonce) script.setAttribute('nonce', nonce);
  document.head.appendChild(script);
  
  script.onload = () => {
    window.getNexoState.scriptsLoaded = true;
    window.getNexoState.scriptsLoading = false;
    console.log('Script de modais carregado via HomeHero');
  };
}

// Modificar o botão para carregar o script dinamicamente
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  const demoButton = document.getElementById('hero-demo-btn');
  if (demoButton) {
    demoButton.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      // Carrega o script se ainda não foi carregado
      if (!window.getNexoState || !window.getNexoState.scriptsLoaded) {
        loadModalScripts();
        // Aguarda um pouco para o script carregar antes de chamar a função
        setTimeout(() => {
          if (typeof window.abrirModalDemo === 'function') {
            window.abrirModalDemo();
          } else {
            // Se o script ainda não estiver pronto, tenta novamente
            setTimeout(() => {
              if (typeof window.abrirModalDemo === 'function') {
                window.abrirModalDemo();
              } else {
                alert('Demo chat ainda em setup – em breve!');
              }
            }, 500);
          }
        }, 300);
      } else {
        // Script já carregado, chama a função diretamente
        if (typeof window.abrirModalDemo === 'function') {
          window.abrirModalDemo();
        } else {
          alert('Demo chat ainda em setup – em breve!');
        }
      }
    });
  }
});

  (function() {
    const initSlider = () => {
      const track = document.getElementById('slider-track');
      const prevBtn = document.getElementById('carousel-prev');
      const nextBtn = document.getElementById('carousel-next');
      const dots = document.querySelectorAll('#slider-dots .dot');
      
      if (!track || !prevBtn || !nextBtn) return;

      let currentIndex = 0;
      const totalItems = track.children.length;
      let timer = null;

      const getItemsVisible = () => {
        if (window.innerWidth < 768) return 1;
        if (window.innerWidth < 1100) return 2;
        return 3;
      };

      const updatePos = () => {
        const visible = getItemsVisible();
        const maxScroll = totalItems - visible;
        
        if (currentIndex < 0) currentIndex = maxScroll;
        if (currentIndex > maxScroll) currentIndex = 0;

        const card = track.children[0];
        const width = card.offsetWidth;
        const gap = 32;
        const offset = currentIndex * (width + gap);

        track.style.transform = `translateX(-${offset}px)`;

        // Dots
        dots.forEach((dot, i) => {
          dot.classList.toggle('active', i === currentIndex);
        });
      };

      const moveNext = () => {
        currentIndex++;
        updatePos();
      };

      const movePrev = () => {
        currentIndex--;
        updatePos();
      };

      const startAuto = () => {
        stopAuto();
        timer = setInterval(moveNext, 5000);
      };

      const stopAuto = () => {
        if (timer) clearInterval(timer);
      };

      nextBtn.onclick = () => { moveNext(); startAuto(); };
      prevBtn.onclick = () => { movePrev(); startAuto(); };

      dots.forEach((dot, i) => {
        dot.onclick = () => {
          currentIndex = i;
          updatePos();
          startAuto();
        };
      });

      track.onmouseenter = stopAuto;
      track.onmouseleave = startAuto;

      window.onresize = updatePos;
      
      startAuto();
      updatePos();
    };

    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
      document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initSlider);
    } else {
      initSlider();
    }
  })();
</script>  <div id="roi-calculator" class="roi-calculator" data-astro-cid-h6rwxm4f> <div class="roi-header" data-astro-cid-h6rwxm4f> <h3 class="roi-title" data-astro-cid-h6rwxm4f>Calculadora de ROI   function calculateROI() {
    const attendants = parseInt(document.getElementById('current-attendants').value) || 0;
    const salary = parseInt(document.getElementById('avg-salary').value) || 0;
    const hours = parseInt(document.getElementById('work-hours').value) || 0;
    const customers = parseInt(document.getElementById('monthly-customers').value) || 0;

    // Cálculos
    const monthlyLaborCost = attendants * salary;
    const dailyCoverage = attendants * hours;
    const monthlySavings = monthlyLaborCost * 0.7; // 70% economia estimada
    const sixMonthSavings = monthlySavings * 6;
    const roi = monthlySavings > 0 ? ((monthlySavings / 97) * 100) : 0;

    // Atualizar resultados com animação
    updateResult('monthly-saving', `R$ ${monthlySavings.toLocaleString('pt-BR')}`);
    updateResult('six-month-saving', `R$ ${sixMonthSavings.toLocaleString('pt-BR')}`);
    updateResult('hours-coverage', `${dailyCoverage * 30}h`);
    updateResult('roi-percentage', `${Math.round(roi)}%`);
  }

  function updateResult(elementId, value) {
    const element = document.getElementById(elementId);
    if (element) {
      element.classList.add('result-updating');
      setTimeout(() => {
        element.textContent = value;
        element.classList.remove('result-updating');
      }, 150);
    }
  }

  function scrollToPricing() {
    const pricingSection = document.querySelector('.pricing-section, #pricing, [data-section="pricing"]');
    if (pricingSection) {
      pricingSection.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
    } else {
      // Fallback: scroll to bottom where pricing usually is
      window.scrollTo({ top: document.body.scrollHeight, behavior: 'smooth' });
    }
  }

  // Initialize calculation and event listeners
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    // Calculate initial values
    calculateROI();

    // Add event listeners to all inputs
    const inputs = document.querySelectorAll('#roi-calculator input');
    inputs.forEach(input => {
      input.addEventListener('input', calculateROI);
      input.addEventListener('change', calculateROI);
    });

    // Add visual feedback
    inputs.forEach(input => {
      input.addEventListener('focus', function() {
        this.parentElement.style.transform = 'scale(1.02)';
      });

      input.addEventListener('blur', function() {
        this.parentElement.style.transform = 'scale(1)';
      });
    });
    
    // Add click listener for the CTA button (replacing inline onclick)
    const roiCtaBtn = document.getElementById('roi-cta-btn-element');
    if (roiCtaBtn) roiCtaBtn.addEventListener('click', scrollToPricing);
  });

  // Make functions globally available
  window.calculateROI = calculateROI;
  window.scrollToPricing = scrollToPricing;
</script> <!-- Giant Features Showcase --><section class="giant-features" data-astro-cid-hx3p4jem> <!-- Magic Replies --> <div class="giant-feature-row animate-on-scroll" data-astro-cid-hx3p4jem> <div     const closeIaBtn = document.getElementById('close-ia-modal-btn');
    if (closeIaBtn) {

          window.fecharModalIA();
        }
      });
    }

    // Send IA Message
    const sendIaBtn = document.getElementById('send-ia-msg-btn');
    if (sendIaBtn) {
      sendIaBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (typeof window.sendMessageIA === 'function') {
          window.sendMessageIA();
        }
      });
    }

    // IA Voice
    const voiceIaBtn = document.getElementById('start-ia-voice-btn');
    if (voiceIaBtn) {
      voiceIaBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (typeof window.iniciarVozIA === 'function') {
          window.iniciarVozIA();
        }
      });
    }

    const iaInput = document.getElementById('ia-chat-input');
    if (iaInput) {

          window.handleKeyPressIA(e);
        }
      });
    }

          window.preencherMensagemIA(msg);
        }
      });
    });

    // Show Product Buttons
    const productBtns = [
      document.getElementById('show-product-car-btn'),
      document.getElementById('show-product-phone-btn'),
      document.getElementById('show-product-pc-btn')
    ];
    productBtns.forEach(btn => {
      if (btn) {
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          const type = btn.getAttribute('data-type');
          const index = parseInt(btn.getAttribute('data-index'));
          if (type && typeof window.mostrarProdutoIA === 'function') {
            window.mostrarProdutoIA(type, index);
          }
        });
      }
    });

    // Close Product Modal
    const closeProductBtn = document.getElementById('close-product-modal-ia-btn');
    if (closeProductBtn) {
      closeProductBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (typeof window.fecharModalProdutoIA === 'function') {
          window.fecharModalProdutoIA();
        }
      });
    }
  });

    (function(d,t) {
      const loadWidget = () => {
        const isMobile = window.innerWidth < 768;
        // Atraso significativamente maior para não bloquear o LCP
        const delay = isMobile ? 12000 : 6000; // Aumentado para reduzir impacto no LCP
        // delay: 12000 - otimização avançada para mobile

        setTimeout(() => {
          // Widget habilitado
          // Verificar se o servidor da API está acessível antes de tentar carregar
          fetch('/widget.js', {
            method: 'HEAD',
            mode: 'no-cors',
            cache: 'force-cache'
          }).then((response) => {
            if (response.ok || response.type === 'opaque') {
              var g=d.createElement(t),s=d.getElementsByTagName(t)[0];
              g.src="/widget.js?v=" + Date.now();
              g.defer = true;
              g.async = true;
              g.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
              g.setAttribute('data-bot', 'central');
              g.fetchpriority = 'low';
              g.setAttribute('loading', 'lazy');

              g.onerror = function() {
                console.warn('[Widget] GetNexo widget failed to load - API unavailable');
              };

              s.parentNode.insertBefore(g,s);
            } else {
              console.warn('[Widget] GetNexo API returned error status:', response.status);
            }
          }).catch((error) => {
            console.warn('[Widget] GetNexo API unavailable:', error.message);
          });
        }, delay);
      };

      // Carregar apenas quando o usuário interagir com a página
      if (document.readyState === 'complete') {
        loadWidget();
      } else {
        window.addEventListener('load', loadWidget);
      }
      
      // Adicionar interatividade para carregar widget quando usuário precisar
      let userInteracted = false;
      const interactionEvents = ['click', 'touchstart', 'keydown', 'scroll'];
      
      interactionEvents.forEach(event => {
        document.addEventListener(event, () => {
          if (!userInteracted) {
            userInteracted = true;
            loadWidget();
          }
        }, { once: true, passive: true });
      });
    })(document,"script");

    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
      const menuBtn = document.getElementById('mobile-menu-btn');
      const mobileMenu = document.getElementById('mobile-menu');
      const mobileClose = document.getElementById('mobile-close');
      const header = document.getElementById('main-nav');
      const backToTopBtn = document.getElementById('back-to-top');

      // Otimização: evitar reflow durante interações
      // Adicionar transform: translateZ(0) para forçar hardware acceleration
      if (header) {
        header.style.transform = 'translateZ(0)';
        header.style.backfaceVisibility = 'hidden';
      }

      if (menuBtn && mobileMenu) {
        const toggleMobile = () => {
          menuBtn.classList.toggle('active');
          mobileMenu.classList.toggle('active');
          document.body.style.overflow = mobileMenu.classList.contains('active') ? 'hidden' : '';
        };
        menuBtn.addEventListener('click', toggleMobile);
        mobileClose?.addEventListener('click', toggleMobile);

        mobileMenu.querySelectorAll('a').forEach(link => {
          link.addEventListener('click', () => {
            menuBtn.classList.remove('active');
            mobileMenu.classList.remove('active');
            document.body.style.overflow = '';
          });
        });
      }

      // Header Scroll & Back to Top logic
      let ticking = false;
      let lastScrollY = window.scrollY;

      const updateHeader = () => {
        const currentScrollY = window.scrollY;
        if (header) {
          if (currentScrollY > 20) header.classList.add('scrolled');
          else header.classList.remove('scrolled');
        }
        
        if (backToTopBtn) {
          if (currentScrollY > 500) backToTopBtn.classList.add('visible');
          else backToTopBtn.classList.remove('visible');
        }
        lastScrollY = currentScrollY;
        ticking = false;
      };

      window.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
        if (!ticking) {
          window.requestAnimationFrame(updateHeader);
          ticking = true;
        }
      }, { passive: true });

      // Parallax Mouse Glow for Header
      let mouseTicking = false;
      let headerRect = null;

      const updateHeaderRect = () => {
        if (header) headerRect = header.getBoundingClientRect();
      };

      // Initial rect and update on resize
      updateHeaderRect();
      window.addEventListener('resize', updateHeaderRect, { passive: true });

      header?.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
        if (!mouseTicking) {
          window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
            if (!headerRect) updateHeaderRect();
            const { left, top, width, height } = headerRect;
            const x = ((e.clientX - left) / width) * 100;
            const y = ((e.clientY - top) / height) * 100;
            header.style.setProperty('--mouse-x', `${x}%`);
            header.style.setProperty('--mouse-y', `${y}%`);
            header.style.background = `radial-gradient(circle at ${x}% ${y}%, rgba(0, 212, 255, 0.15) 0%, rgba(3, 7, 18, 0.8) 50%)`;
            mouseTicking = false;
          });
          mouseTicking = true;
        }
      }, { passive: true });
      header?.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => { if(header) header.style.background = ''; });
      header?.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => { if(header) header.style.background = ''; });

      // Back to Top Click
      backToTopBtn?.addEventListener('click', () => {
        window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
      });
    });
  </script> <!-- Carregamento otimizado de feriados - totalmente não bloqueante --> <script nonce="9Vq/Kfn10b5K09mw5QdpHg==" defer>
    // Verificação de feriados otimizada - executada apenas quando o sistema está ocioso
    // Não bloqueia a linha de execução principal
    (function() {
      const checkHolidays = () => {
        const hoje = new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
        
        // Usa cache do navegador para evitar requisições repetidas
        if (window.getnexoHolidayCache && window.getnexoHolidayCache.date === hoje) {
          if (window.getnexoHolidayCache.hasHoliday) {
            document.body.classList.add('feriado-ativo');
          }
          return;
        }

        // Fetch otimizado com timeout
        const controller = new AbortController();
        const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 3000);

        fetch('/data/feriados.json', {
          cache: 'force-cache',
          signal: controller.signal
        })
        .then(response => {
          clearTimeout(timeoutId);
          return response.json();
        })
        .then(data => {
          const feriadoHoje = data.feriados.find(f => f.data === hoje);
          const hasHoliday = !!feriadoHoje;
          
          // Cache no localStorage para evitar requisições futuras
          try {
            localStorage.setItem('getnexo-holiday-cache', JSON.stringify({
              date: hoje,
              hasHoliday: hasHoliday,
              timestamp: Date.now()
            }));
          } catch (e) {
            // Ignora erros de localStorage
          }
          
          if (hasHoliday) {
            document.body.classList.add('feriado-ativo');
          }
        })
        .catch(() => {
          // Silenciosamente falha - feriados não são críticos
        });
      };

      // Executa apenas quando o sistema está ocioso
      if ('requestIdleCallback' in window) {
        window.requestIdleCallback(checkHolidays, { timeout: 2000 });
      } else {
        // Fallback não bloqueante
        setTimeout(checkHolidays, 3000);
      }
    })();
  </script> <script nonce="9Vq/Kfn10b5K09mw5QdpHg==" defer>
    function mostrarNotificacaoFeriado(feriado) {
      const notification = document.createElement('div');
      notification.className = 'feriado-notification glass-panel';
      const htmlContent = `
        <div class="feriado-content">
          <div class="feriado-emoji">🎉</div>
          <div class="feriado-text">
            <h4>${feriado.idiomas.pt}</h4>
            <p>${feriado.bot_frase.pt}</p>
          </div>
          <button class="feriado-close">×</button>
        </div>
      `;

      if (window.trustedTypes && window.trustedTypes.createHTML) {
        notification.innerHTML = window.trustedTypes.createHTML(htmlContent);
      } else if (window.getnexoPolicy && window.getnexoPolicy.createHTML) {
        notification.innerHTML = window.getnexoPolicy.createHTML(htmlContent);
      } else {
        notification.innerHTML = htmlContent;
      }

      const closeBtn = notification.querySelector('.feriado-close');
      if (closeBtn) {
        closeBtn.addEventListener('click', () => notification.remove());
      }

      document.body.appendChild(notification);

      // Auto-remover após 10 segundos
      setTimeout(() => {
        if (notification.parentElement) {
          notification.remove();
        }
      }, 10000);
    }
  </script> <!-- Geração de breadcrumbs - carregado após o DOM --> <script defer nonce="9Vq/Kfn10b5K09mw5QdpHg==">
    function generateBreadcrumbs() {
      const path = window.location.pathname;
      const segments = path.split('/').filter(segment => segment !== '');

      // Breadcrumb mappings for better names
      const breadcrumbMap = {
        'chat-ia': 'Chat Inteligente',
        'sistema-tickets': 'Sistema de Tickets',
        'analise-sentimento': 'Análise de Sentimento',
        'magic-replies': 'Magic Replies',
        'gamificacao-vendas': 'Gamificação de Vendas',
        'programa-pontos': 'Programa de Pontos',
        'documentacao': 'Documentação',
        'api': 'API',
        'integracoes': 'Integrações',
        'suporte': 'Suporte',
        'faq': 'Perguntas Frequentes',
        'blog': 'Blog',
        'admin': 'Administração'
      };

      const breadcrumbNav = document.getElementById('breadcrumb-nav');
      const breadcrumbList = document.querySelector('.breadcrumb-list');

      if (segments.length === 0) {
        breadcrumbNav.style.display = 'none';
        return;
      }

      breadcrumbNav.style.display = 'block';

      // Clear existing breadcrumbs except home
      while (breadcrumbList.children.length > 1) {
        breadcrumbList.removeChild(breadcrumbList.lastChild);
      }

      let currentPath = '';

      segments.forEach((segment, index) => {
        currentPath += '/' + segment;

        const listItem = document.createElement('li');
        listItem.className = 'breadcrumb-item';

        const link = document.createElement('a');
        link.href = currentPath;
        link.className = 'breadcrumb-link';

        // Check if it's the last segment (current page)
        if (index === segments.length - 1) {
          link.classList.add('active');
          link.setAttribute('aria-current', 'page');
        }

        // Use mapped name or capitalize segment
        const displayName = breadcrumbMap[segment] ||
          segment.split('-').map(word =>
            word.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + word.slice(1)
          ).join(' ');

        link.textContent = displayName;
        listItem.appendChild(link);
        breadcrumbList.appendChild(listItem);
      });
    }

    // Generate breadcrumbs on page load and when navigating (for SPA-like behavior)
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', generateBreadcrumbs);
    window.addEventListener('popstate', generateBreadcrumbs);
  </script> <!-- Botão de voltar ao topo - carregado após o DOM --> <button id="back-to-top" class="back-to-top" aria-label="Voltar ao topo"> <svg width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"> <path d="M18 15l-6-6-6 6"></path> </svg> </button>  <div id="discovery-hub" class="discovery-hub"> <div class="hub-container glass-panel reveal"> <div class="hub-header"> <h3 class="text-gradient">Nexbot Discovery Hub</h3> <button id="hub-close" class="hub-close">×</button> </div> <div class="hub-body"> <p class="hub-intro">Explore as ferramentas que estão transformando o WhatsApp em máquinas de lucro.</p> <div class="hub-features"> <div class="hub-feature" data-tour="checkout"> <span class="hub-icon">💰</span> <div class="hub-info"> <strong>Checkout PIX</strong> <span>Venda no chat em 2 min</span> </div> </div> <div class="hub-feature" data-tour="ia"> <span class="hub-icon">🧠</span> <div class="hub-info"> <strong>IA Generativa</strong> <span>Treine seu bot com PDFs</span> </div> </div> <div class="hub-feature" data-tour="ads"> <span class="hub-icon">🚀</span> <div class="hub-info"> <strong>Click-to-WhatsApp</strong> <span>Anúncios que convertem</span> </div> </div> </div> <div class="hub-footer"> <a href="/criar-bot" class="btn-primary-glow w-full">Ativar Todas as Soluções</a> </div> </div> </div> </div> <!-- Global Floating UI - Left (Unified Accessibility & Tour) --> <!-- Carregamento lazy para não bloquear o LCP --> <div class="dock-left">  <script nonce="9Vq/Kfn10b5K09mw5QdpHg==" defer type="module">
  // Load Shepherd CSS safely (avoid inline onload handlers)
  const cssLink = document.createElement('link');
  cssLink.rel = 'stylesheet';
  cssLink.href = 'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/shepherd.js@13.0.0/dist/css/shepherd.css';
  document.head.appendChild(cssLink);

  // Lazy load Shepherd.js only when needed
  let Shepherd = null;
  let tourInstance = null;
  let tourLoaded = false;

  const targetPage = document.querySelector('[data-tour-target]')?.getAttribute('data-tour-target') || 'home';
  const autoStart = document.querySelector('[data-tour-autostart]')?.getAttribute('data-tour-autostart') === 'true';
  const mobileOptimized = document.querySelector('[data-tour-mobile]')?.getAttribute('data-tour-mobile') !== 'false';

  // Function to load Shepherd.js dynamically
  async function loadShepherd() {
    if (tourLoaded) return;
    
    try {
      const module = await import('https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/shepherd.js@13.0.0/+esm');
      Shepherd = module.default;
      window.Shepherd = Shepherd;
      tourLoaded = true;
      console.log('Shepherd.js loaded successfully');
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to load Shepherd.js:', error);
      return null;
    }
  }

  function createTour() {
    // Detect mobile
    const isMobile = window.innerWidth <= 768;
    const useMobileMode = mobileOptimized && isMobile;

    // Tour configuration
    const tourConfig = {
      useModalOverlay: true,
      defaultStepOptions: {
        classes: 'shepherd-theme-arrows shepherd-theme-dark',
        scrollTo: { behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' },
        cancelIcon: { enabled: true },
        arrow: !useMobileMode, // No arrows on mobile
        modalOverlayOpeningPadding: useMobileMode ? 4 : 8,
        modalOverlayOpeningRadius: useMobileMode ? 8 : 16,
        popperOptions: {
          modifiers: [
            { name: 'offset', options: { offset: useMobileMode ? [0, 8] : [0, 16] } }
          ]
        }
      }
    };

    if (!Shepherd) {
      console.warn('Shepherd.js not loaded, tour disabled');
      return null;
    }
    tourInstance = new Shepherd.Tour(tourConfig);

    // Add steps based on target page
    if (targetPage === 'home') {
      addHomeSteps(useMobileMode);
    } else if (targetPage === 'demo') {
      addDemoSteps(useMobileMode);
    } else if (targetPage === 'pricing') {
      addPricingSteps(useMobileMode);
    }

    // Auto-start if configured
    if (autoStart) {
      // Small delay to ensure DOM is ready
      setTimeout(() => {
        tourInstance.start();
      }, 1000);
    }

    return tourInstance;
  }

  // Progress indicator
  if (tourInstance) {
    tourInstance.on('show', function(event) {
    const currentStep = event.step;
    const totalSteps = tourInstance.steps.length;
    const currentIndex = tourInstance.steps.indexOf(currentStep) + 1;

    // Add progress indicator to step content
    const progressHtml = `<div class="tour-progress" style="margin-top: 1rem; font-size: 0.8rem; color: #666;">
      Passo ${currentIndex} de ${totalSteps}
      <div style="width: 100%; height: 4px; background: #eee; margin-top: 0.5rem; border-radius: 2px;">
        <div style="width: ${(currentIndex / totalSteps) * 100}%; height: 100%; background: linear-gradient(90deg, #00d4ff, #00ff9d); border-radius: 2px; transition: width 0.3s ease;"></div>
      </div>
    </div>`;

    // Insert progress after the main text
    setTimeout(() => {
      const stepElement = currentStep.el;
      if (stepElement && !stepElement.querySelector('.tour-progress')) {
        const textElement = stepElement.querySelector('.shepherd-text');
        if (textElement) {
          textElement.insertAdjacentHTML('afterend', progressHtml);
        }
      }
    }, 10);
    });
  }

  // Global function to start tour - Lazy load Shepherd.js
  window.startGetNexoTour = async function() {
    // Load Shepherd.js if not loaded
    if (!Shepherd) {
      console.log('Loading Shepherd.js...');
      await loadShepherd();
    }
    
    // Create tour if not created
    if (!tourInstance) {
      console.log('Creating tour...');
      createTour();
    }
    
    // Start tour
    if (tourInstance) {
      tourInstance.start();
    } else {
      console.error('Failed to start tour');
    }
  };

  // Initialize on DOM ready - Only set up event listeners
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    // Set up event listener for tour button
    document.getElementById('tour-btn')?.addEventListener('click', async () => {
      console.log('Tour button clicked - starting tour...');
      await window.startGetNexoTour();
    });
    
    // Auto-start if configured (lazy load)
    if (autoStart) {
      // Small delay to ensure DOM is ready
      setTimeout(async () => {
        console.log('Auto-starting tour...');
        await window.startGetNexoTour();
      }, 1000);
    }
  });

  window.getNexoTour = {
    start: () => window.startGetNexoTour(),
    cancel: () => tourInstance?.cancel(),
    complete: () => tourInstance?.complete()

  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    // Elements
    const trigger = document.getElementById('acc-trigger');
    const panel = document.getElementById('acc-panel');
    const closeBtn = document.getElementById('acc-close');
    const resetBtn = document.getElementById('acc-reset');
    const zoomDisplay = document.getElementById('zoom-display');
    const readingGuide = document.getElementById('reading-guide-line');
    const tourTrigger = document.getElementById('trigger-tour');

    // State
    const state = {
      zoom: 100,
      font: false,
      highlight: false,
      cursor: false,
      mono: false,
      contrast: false,
      motion: false,
      guide: false
    };

    // --- Helper Functions ---
    const toggleClass = (cls, active) => {
      active ? document.body.classList.add(cls) : document.body.classList.remove(cls);
    };

    const updateBtn = (id, active) => {
      const btn = document.getElementById(id);
      if(btn) active ? btn.classList.add('active') : btn.classList.remove('active');
    };

    const setZoom = (val) => {
      // Range 80% to 150% is safer for rem scaling without breaking visuals too much
      const newZoom = Math.min(Math.max(val, 80), 160);
      state.zoom = newZoom;
      if (zoomDisplay) zoomDisplay.textContent = newZoom + '%';
      
      // Use Root Font Size scaling (standard a11y approach)
      // Default browser font size is usually 16px (100%).
      // We scale this percentage.
      document.documentElement.style.fontSize = `${newZoom}%`;
      
      // Remove old transform potential leftovers if they exist
      document.body.style.transform = '';
      document.body.style.transformOrigin = '';
      document.body.style.width = '';
    };

    // --- Event Listeners ---

    // Panel Toggle
    if (trigger) trigger.onclick = (e) => { e.stopPropagation(); panel.classList.toggle('open'); };
    if (closeBtn) closeBtn.onclick = (e) => { e.stopPropagation(); panel.classList.remove('open'); };
    document.onclick = (e) => {
      if (panel && panel.classList.contains('open') && !panel.contains(e.target) && !trigger.contains(e.target)) {
        panel.classList.remove('open');
      }
    };

    // Tour Trigger (Integrated)
    if (tourTrigger) tourTrigger.onclick = () => {
       if (window.startGetNexoTour) {
         window.startGetNexoTour();
         panel.classList.remove('open'); // Close panel when tour starts
       } else {
         console.warn('Tour function not ready yet');
       }
    };

    // Zoom
    document.getElementById('zoom-in')?.addEventListener('click', () => setZoom(state.zoom + 10));
    document.getElementById('zoom-out')?.addEventListener('click', () => setZoom(state.zoom - 10));

    // Dyslexia Font
    document.getElementById('toggle-font')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      state.font = !state.font;
      toggleClass('acc-dyslexia', state.font);
      updateBtn('toggle-font', state.font);
    });

    // Highlight Links
    document.getElementById('toggle-highlight')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      state.highlight = !state.highlight;
      toggleClass('acc-highlight-links', state.highlight);
      updateBtn('toggle-highlight', state.highlight);
    });

    // Big Cursor
    document.getElementById('toggle-cursor')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      state.cursor = !state.cursor;
      toggleClass('acc-cursor', state.cursor);
      updateBtn('toggle-cursor', state.cursor);
    });

    // Monochrome
    document.getElementById('toggle-mono')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      state.mono = !state.mono;
      toggleClass('acc-monochrome', state.mono);
      updateBtn('toggle-mono', state.mono);
    });

    // High Contrast
    document.getElementById('toggle-contrast')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      state.contrast = !state.contrast;
      toggleClass('high-contrast', state.contrast);
      updateBtn('toggle-contrast', state.contrast);
    });

    // Stop Motion
    document.getElementById('toggle-motion')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      state.motion = !state.motion;
      toggleClass('acc-stop-motion', state.motion);
      updateBtn('toggle-motion', state.motion);
    });

    // Reading Guide
    const toggleGuide = document.getElementById('toggle-guide');
    if (toggleGuide) {
      toggleGuide.onchange = (e) => {
        state.guide = e.target.checked;
        if (state.guide) {
          readingGuide.classList.add('active');
          document.addEventListener('mousemove', moveGuide);
        } else {
          readingGuide.classList.remove('active');
          document.removeEventListener('mousemove', moveGuide);
        }
      };
    }

    const moveGuide = (e) => {
      if (readingGuide) readingGuide.style.top = e.clientY + 'px';
    };

    // Reset All
    if (resetBtn) resetBtn.onclick = () => {
      state.zoom = 100;
      setZoom(100);

      const features = [
        { key: 'font', cls: 'acc-dyslexia', id: 'toggle-font' },
        { key: 'highlight', cls: 'acc-highlight-links', id: 'toggle-highlight' },
        { key: 'cursor', cls: 'acc-cursor', id: 'toggle-cursor' },
        { key: 'mono', cls: 'acc-monochrome', id: 'toggle-mono' },
        { key: 'contrast', cls: 'high-contrast', id: 'toggle-contrast' },
        { key: 'motion', cls: 'acc-stop-motion', id: 'toggle-motion' }
      ];

      features.forEach(f => {
        state[f.key] = false;
        document.body.classList.remove(f.cls);
        updateBtn(f.id, false);
      });

      // Reset Guide
      state.guide = false;
      if (toggleGuide) toggleGuide.checked = false;
      if (readingGuide) readingGuide.classList.remove('active');
      document.removeEventListener('mousemove', moveGuide);

      // Clean styles
      document.documentElement.style.fontSize = ''; 
      document.body.style.transform = '';
      document.body.style.width = '';
    };

  });

  // Carregamento dinâmico do script de modais apenas quando necessário
  function loadModalScripts() {
    if (!window.getNexoState || window.getNexoState.scriptsLoaded || window.getNexoState.scriptsLoading) return;
    
    window.getNexoState.scriptsLoading = true;
    
    // Carrega o script de modais dinamicamente
    const script = document.createElement('script');
    script.src = '/custom/js/modals-demo.js';
    script.defer = true;
    if (Astro.locals.nonce) script.setAttribute('nonce', Astro.locals.nonce);
    document.head.appendChild(script);
    
    script.onload = () => {
      window.getNexoState.scriptsLoaded = true;
      window.getNexoState.scriptsLoading = false;
      console.log('Script de modais carregado via ChatFAB');
    };
  }
  
  document.getElementById('chat-fab').addEventListener('click', () => {
    // Carrega o script se ainda não foi carregado
    if (!window.getNexoState || !window.getNexoState.scriptsLoaded) {
      loadModalScripts();
      // Aguarda um pouco para o script carregar antes de chamar a função
      setTimeout(() => {
        if (typeof window.abrirModalDemo === 'function') {
          window.abrirModalDemo();
        } else {
          // Se o script ainda não estiver pronto, tenta novamente
          setTimeout(() => {
            if (typeof window.abrirModalDemo === 'function') {
              window.abrirModalDemo();
            } else {
              alert('Demo chat ainda em setup – em breve!');
            }
          }, 500);
        }
      }, 300);
    } else {
      // Script já carregado, chama a função diretamente
      if (typeof window.abrirModalDemo === 'function') {
        window.abrirModalDemo();
      } else {
        alert('Demo chat ainda em setup – em breve!');
      }
    }
  });

  /* interface Window removed for JS compatibility */

  class CookieConsentManager {
    constructor() {
      this.banner = document.getElementById('cookie-consent-banner');
      this.details = document.getElementById('cookie-details');
      this.btnDetails = document.getElementById('btn-details');
      this.btnSave = document.getElementById('save-prefs');
      this.actions = document.querySelector('.cookie-actions');
      this.detailsOpen = false;

      this.consent = this.getStoredConsent();

      if (!this.consent) {
        this.showBanner();
        this.bindEvents();
      } else {
        this.applyConsent(this.consent);
      }
    }

    showBanner() {
      if(this.banner) this.banner.style.display = 'block';
    }

    bindEvents() {
      // Toggle Details
      if (this.btnDetails) {
        this.btnDetails.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
          e.preventDefault();
          this.toggleDetails();
        });
      }

      // Accept All
      const acceptAll = document.getElementById('accept-all');
      if (acceptAll) acceptAll.addEventListener('click', () => {
        this.setConsent({ essential: true, analytics: true, preference: true, timestamp: Date.now() });
      });

      // Reject All (Non-essential)
      const rejectAll = document.getElementById('reject-all');
      if (rejectAll) rejectAll.addEventListener('click', () => {
        this.setConsent({ essential: true, analytics: false, preference: false, timestamp: Date.now() });
      });

      // Save Preferences (only visible when details open)
      if (this.btnSave) {
        this.btnSave.addEventListener('click', () => {
          const analytics = document.getElementById('analytics-cookies').checked;
          const preference = document.getElementById('preference-cookies').checked;
          this.setConsent({ essential: true, analytics, preference, timestamp: Date.now() });
        });
      }

      // Close Button (X)
      const closeBtn = document.getElementById('close-cookie-banner');
      if (closeBtn) {
        closeBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          if (this.banner) this.banner.style.display = 'none';
        });
      }
    }

    toggleDetails() {
      this.detailsOpen = !this.detailsOpen;
      if (this.detailsOpen && this.details && this.btnDetails) {
        this.details.classList.remove('hidden');
        this.btnDetails.textContent = 'Menos opções';
        if(this.btnSave) this.btnSave.style.display = 'block';
        if(this.actions) this.actions.style.display = 'none';
      } else if (this.details && this.btnDetails) {
        this.details.classList.add('hidden');
        this.btnDetails.textContent = 'Personalizar';
        if(this.btnSave) this.btnSave.style.display = 'none';
        if(this.actions) this.actions.style.display = 'flex';
      }
    }

    setConsent(consent) {
      console.log('[CookieConsent] setConsent called with:', consent);
      localStorage.setItem('cookie-consent', JSON.stringify(consent));
      this.consent = consent;
      if (this.banner) this.banner.style.display = 'none';
      this.applyConsent(consent);

      const win = window;
      if (consent.analytics && win.gtag) {
        console.log('[CookieConsent] Updating gtag consent');
        win.gtag('consent', 'update', { analytics_storage: 'granted' });
      }
    }

    getStoredConsent() {
      const stored = localStorage.getItem('cookie-consent');
      return stored ? JSON.parse(stored) : null;
    }

    applyConsent(consent) {
      const win = window;
      if (!consent.analytics) {
        win['ga-disable-G-F8P994EX00'] = true;
        if (win.gtag) win.gtag('consent', 'default', { analytics_storage: 'denied' });
      }
      if (!consent.preference) {
        localStorage.removeItem('nexo-a11y-settings');
      }
    }
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => new CookieConsentManager());
  } else {
    new CookieConsentManager();
  }
</script> <!-- Skip Link para acessibilidade --><a href="#main-content" class="skip-link sr-only focus:not-sr-only focus:absolute focus:top-4 focus:left-4 bg-blue-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded z-50" data-astro-cid-m5ojr247>

  // PreSupportModal v2.0 - Estado Reativo e Acessibilidade WCAG 2.2 AA
  class PreSupportModal {
    constructor() {
      this.modal = document.getElementById('pre-support-modal');
      this.closeBtn = document.getElementById('close-modal');
      this.textBtn = document.getElementById('btn-text');
      this.voiceBtn = document.getElementById('btn-voice');
      this.voiceStatus = document.getElementById('voice-status');
      this.voiceFeedbackText = document.getElementById('voice-feedback-text');
      this.recognition = null;
      this.isOpen = false;
      this.mode = null;

      this.config = {
        primaryColor: '#00ffa3',
        language: 'pt-BR',
        voiceTimeout: 30000,
        enableAnalytics: true,
        ...(window.preSupportModalConfig || {}) // removed 'as any'
      };

      this.init();
    }

    init() {
      if (!this.modal) return;

      this.bindEvents();
      this.setupKeyboardNavigation();
      this.bindEvents();
      this.setupKeyboardNavigation();
      // this.setupAutoShow(); // Desabilitado para não incomodar

      // Configuração global
      window.modalInstance = this;
    }

    bindEvents() {
      // Botão fechar
      this.closeBtn?.addEventListener('click', () => this.close());

      // Botões de modo
      this.textBtn?.addEventListener('click', () => this.selectText());
      this.voiceBtn?.addEventListener('click', () => this.selectVoice());

      // ESC para fechar
      document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
        if (e.key === 'Escape' && this.isOpen) {
          this.close();
        }
      });

      // Click no overlay para fechar
      this.modal?.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        if (e.target === this.modal) {
          this.close();
        }
      });
    }

    setupKeyboardNavigation() {
      const focusableElements = this.modal?.querySelectorAll(
        'button, [href], input, select, textarea, [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])'
      );

      if (focusableElements) {
        const firstElement = focusableElements[0];
        const lastElement = focusableElements[focusableElements.length - 1];

        this.modal?.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
          if (e.key === 'Tab') {
            if (e.shiftKey) {
              if (document.activeElement === firstElement) {
                lastElement.focus();
                e.preventDefault();
              }
            } else {
              if (document.activeElement === lastElement) {
                firstElement.focus();
                e.preventDefault();
              }
            }
          }
        });
      }
    }

    setupAutoShow() {
      // Mostrar modal após delay, respeitando preferências do usuário
      const prefersReducedMotion = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;

      setTimeout(() => {
        if (!prefersReducedMotion && !this.isOpen) {
          this.open();
        }
      }, 2000);
    }

    // API Pública
    open() {
      if (this.modal && !this.isOpen) {
        this.isOpen = true;
        this.modal.classList.remove('hidden');
        this.modal.classList.add('modal-enter');

        // Foco automático no primeiro botão
        setTimeout(() => {
          this.textBtn?.focus();
        }, 100);

        // Disparar evento
        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('preSupportModalOpened'));
      }
    }

    close() {
      if (this.modal && this.isOpen) {
        this.isOpen = false;
        this.modal.classList.add('hidden');
        this.modal.classList.remove('modal-enter');

        // Parar reconhecimento de voz se ativo
        if (this.recognition) {
          this.recognition.stop();
        }

        // Disparar evento
        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('preSupportModalClosed'));
      }
    }

    selectText() {
      this.mode = 'text';
      this.close();
      this.openChat();
    }

    selectVoice() {
      this.mode = 'voice';
      this.startVoiceRecognition();
    }

    startVoiceRecognition() {
      const SpeechRecognition = window.SpeechRecognition || window.webkitSpeechRecognition;

      if (!SpeechRecognition) {
        this.showFallback('Seu navegador não suporta reconhecimento de voz. Use o modo texto.');
        return;
      }

      // Verificar permissões
      if (navigator.permissions) {
        navigator.permissions.query({ name: 'microphone' }).then((result) => {
          if (result.state === 'denied') {
            this.showFallback('Permissão de microfone negada. Permita o acesso ou use o modo texto.');
            return;
          }
          this.initializeRecognition(SpeechRecognition);
        }).catch(() => {
          this.initializeRecognition(SpeechRecognition);
        });
      } else {
        this.initializeRecognition(SpeechRecognition);
      }
    }

    initializeRecognition(SpeechRecognition) {
      this.recognition = new SpeechRecognition();
      this.recognition.lang = this.config.language;
      this.recognition.interimResults = true;
      this.recognition.maxAlternatives = 1;
      this.recognition.continuous = false;

      this.recognition.onstart = () => {
        this.showVoiceFeedback('Ouvindo...');
      };

      this.recognition.onresult = (event) => {
        const lastResult = event.results[event.results.length - 1];
        const transcript = lastResult[0].transcript;

        this.showVoiceFeedback(transcript);

        if (lastResult.isFinal) {
          setTimeout(() => {
            this.sendVoiceMessage(transcript);
          }, 500);
        }
      };

      this.recognition.onerror = (event) => {
        let message = 'Erro ao reconhecer voz.';
        switch (event.error) {
          case 'no-speech':
            message = 'Nenhuma fala detectada. Tente novamente.';
            break;
          case 'audio-capture':
            message = 'Microfone não encontrado.';
            break;
          case 'not-allowed':
            message = 'Permissão de microfone negada.';
            break;
        }
        this.showFallback(message);
      };

      this.recognition.onend = () => {
        this.hideVoiceFeedback();
      };

      try {
        this.recognition.start();
      } catch (error) {
        this.showFallback('Erro ao iniciar reconhecimento de voz.');
      }
    }

    showVoiceFeedback(text) {
      if (this.voiceStatus && this.voiceFeedbackText) {
        this.voiceStatus.classList.remove('hidden');
        this.voiceFeedbackText.textContent = text;
      }
    }

    hideVoiceFeedback() {
      if (this.voiceStatus) {
        this.voiceStatus.classList.add('hidden');
      }
    }

    showFallback(message) {
      this.hideVoiceFeedback();
      // Poderia mostrar um toast ou modal de erro
      console.warn('PreSupportModal:', message);
      alert(message);
    }

    openChat() {
      const win = window;
      if (win.$chatwoot) {
        win.$chatwoot.toggle();
      } else {
        window.location.href = '/contato';
      }
    }

    async sendVoiceMessage(transcript) {
      try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/support/voice', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            message: transcript,
            userAgent: navigator.userAgent,
            timestamp: Date.now(),
            language: this.config.language
          })
        });

        if (response.ok) {
          this.close();
          this.openChat();
        } else {
          throw new Error('Erro na API');
        }
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('Erro ao enviar mensagem de voz:', error);
        this.showFallback('Erro ao processar mensagem de voz. Tente novamente.');
      }
    }
  }

  // Inicialização
  document.addEventListener('astro:page-load', () => {
    new PreSupportModal();
  });

  if (document.readyState === 'complete') {
    new PreSupportModal();
  } else {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
      new PreSupportModal();
    });
  }
</script>   <!-- Script de otimização de carregamento de imagens e componentes - Otimizado para performance --> <script nonce="9Vq/Kfn10b5K09mw5QdpHg==" defer>
    // Otimização: Lazy loading para imagens e skeleton loading
    // Executado apenas no lado do cliente após o DOM estar pronto
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
      // Otimização: Lazy loading para imagens com IntersectionObserver otimizado
      if ('IntersectionObserver' in window) {
        const lazyImages = document.querySelectorAll('img[loading="lazy"]');
        const imageObserver = new IntersectionObserver((entries, observer) => {
          entries.forEach(entry => {
            if (entry.isIntersecting) {
              const img = entry.target;
              img.classList.add('loaded');
              observer.unobserve(img);
            }
          });
        }, {
          rootMargin: '100px 0px', // Aumentado para carregar imagens antes de entrarem na viewport
          threshold: 0.01
        });

        lazyImages.forEach(img => imageObserver.observe(img));
      }

      // Otimização: Lazy loading para iframes e vídeos
      if ('IntersectionObserver' in window) {
        const lazyElements = document.querySelectorAll('iframe[loading="lazy"], video[loading="lazy"], [data-lazy]');
        const lazyObserver = new IntersectionObserver((entries, observer) => {
          entries.forEach(entry => {
            if (entry.isIntersecting) {
              const element = entry.target;
              
              // Carregar iframe
              if (element.tagName === 'IFRAME') {
                const src = element.dataset.src || element.src;
                if (src && element.src !== src) {
                  element.src = src;
                }
              }
              
              // Carregar vídeo
              if (element.tagName === 'VIDEO') {
                const src = element.dataset.src;
                if (src) {
                  element.src = src;
                  element.load();
                }
              }
              
              // Carregar outros elementos lazy
              if (element.dataset.lazy) {
                const lazyContent = element.dataset.lazy;
                if (lazyContent) {
                  element.innerHTML = lazyContent;
                }
              }
              
              observer.unobserve(element);
            }
          });
        }, {
          rootMargin: '200px 0px', // Carregar mais cedo para elementos pesados
          threshold: 0.01
        });

        lazyElements.forEach(el => lazyObserver.observe(el));
      }

      // Otimização: Skeleton loading para componentes com IntersectionObserver otimizado
      if ('IntersectionObserver' in window) {
        const skeletonElements = document.querySelectorAll('.skeleton-loading');
        const skeletonObserver = new IntersectionObserver((entries, observer) => {
          entries.forEach(entry => {
            if (entry.isIntersecting) {
              const element = entry.target;
              // Simular carregamento de dados
              setTimeout(() => {
                element.classList.remove('skeleton-loading');
                element.classList.add('loaded');
              }, 500);
              observer.unobserve(element);
            }
          });
        }, {
          rootMargin: '100px 0px',
          threshold: 0.01
        });

        skeletonElements.forEach(el => skeletonObserver.observe(el));
      }

      // Otimização: Font loading detection
      if ('fonts' in document) {
        document.fonts.ready.then(() => {
          document.documentElement.classList.add('font-loaded');
          document.documentElement.classList.remove('font-loading');
        });
      }

      // Otimização: Reduzir reflows e repaints
      const optimizeLayout = () => {
        // Forçar hardware acceleration para elementos animados
        const animatedElements = document.querySelectorAll('.animate-on-scroll, .global-header, .btn-start, .logo-box, .nav-links a, .mobile-menu-btn');
        animatedElements.forEach(el => {
          el.style.transform = 'translateZ(0)';
          el.style.backfaceVisibility = 'hidden';
          el.style.willChange = 'transform, opacity';
        });

        // Otimizar propriedades CSS para reduzir repaints
        const criticalElements = document.querySelectorAll('.global-header, .logo-text, .btn-start, .nav-links a');
        criticalElements.forEach(el => {
          el.style.contain = 'layout style paint';
        });
      };

      // Executar otimização após o carregamento inicial
      requestAnimationFrame(optimizeLayout);
    });
  </script> </div> <!-- end of #layout-wrapper --> </body></html>
